```
def project_simple(h,S):
  y=h.copy()
  for _ in range(50):
    y=np.clip(y,0,1)
    diff=S-y.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
    if diff>0:
      # add to entries <1 proportional to room? y += diff*(1-y)/sum(1-y)? could overshoot.
    else:
      y += diff*y/sum(y)
  return y
```
This previous. But exact projection not crucial. For L-BFGS, feasible step with mean projection: y=h - step*g_t; if min<0 or max>1, scale step? Simpler: use SLSQP? Smooth gradient method:
```
def smooth_grad(h, alpha):
  c=correlate(h,1-h,'full')
  F,alpha? 
  w=exp(alpha*(c-cmax)); w /= sum(w)
  Wrev = w[::-1]
  Ch = np.convolve(h, Wrev, mode='full')[:n]
  C1 = np.convolve(ones, Wrev, mode='full')[:n]
  grad = C1 - 2*Ch
  return grad, c
```

For n=2 h=[a,b], c full? c_lags: d=-1: h0(1-h1)? Wait full length3. We can trust.

Simulated annealing might be easiest and effective. Let's design fast binary local search.

Binary objective c for h ∈ {0,1}. Represent as int8. c_u = np.correlate(h.astype(float?), 1-h) but for integer can use full; max integer. Use int32. Bound target. Local moves:
- Flip a 1 to 0 and flip a 0 to 1 (swap) preserves sum and integer. Change in c array: flipping positions p (1->0) and q (0->1). We can update c as sum of individual flip updates. For a single flip at p from val v to 1-v? The change in c_l can be computed quickly. Let's derive update for a bit flip (not swap) and maintain sum via pair.
If bit p changes from v0 to v1. For each lag d:
Pairs affected: as first i=p: term changes from v0(1-h_j) to v1(1-h_j): Δ = (v1-v0)(1-h_{p+d}) if p+d valid.
As second j=p: term changes from h_i(1-v0) to h_i(1-v1): Δ = -(v1-v0)h_{p-d} if p-d valid.
If d=0: both: new v1(1-v1)-v0(1-v0). For flip 0->1, Δ=1 -? h=0 old0, new1*0=0 => Δ=0? Actually F0 term for binary always 0 (h(1-h)=0) for both 0/1, so diagonal Δ=0. Good for binary. For continuous flip, diag changes. For binary, c for diagonal always 0.
For 1->0 also diagonal 0.
Thus update for binary flip has no diagonal effect.
For lag d≠0:
if 0->1: (v1-v0)=1: Δ = (1-h_{p+d}) - h_{p-d}. Valid terms.
if 1->0: Δ = -(1-h_{p+d}) + h_{p-d}.
This can be vectorized over relevant d: positive d p+d valid; negative d p-d valid. In full c indexed l=n-1+d. We can update slices:
For d from -(p) to n-1-p. We can compute delta array over lag offset d maybe. Simpler: For 0->1 at p:
- For positive d (0..n-1-p): Δ_l = 1 - h[p+d] (first)
- For negative d (-(p)..-1): Δ_l = - h[p-d] (second, since p-d valid)
- For d=0: Δ=0.
For 1->0: Δ = - (1-h[p+d]) for pos; Δ = h[p-d] for neg.
This updates slices of c. For a swap (p 1->0, q 0->1) sum preserved: c += update_flip1_to0(p) + update_flip0_to1(q). The diagonal remains zero. Great.

Let's design combined algorithm:
1. Build initial candidates:
   - Parent (continuous) -> candidate A.
   - Hard threshold parent at 0.5 -> binary candidate B. Also threshold with random offsets to preserve mean: for τ in [0.4,0.6], h_b = (h > τ); adjust to exactly sum n/2 by selecting top n/2 values? If parent continuous nonbinary, taking top n/2 values as 1 ensures sum. This is a good binary embedding.
   - Smooth sinusoidal/bump candidates with n same: e.g., h(x)=clip(0.5 + a * f(x)), project to sum. f could be cos, cos-harmonic, Gaussian. But need binary? For continuous.
   - Random binary sequences: start random balanced, then local improve maybe. But time.

Let's implement exact binary local search with time guard.

Binary update functions:
```
def make_binary(h,n, rng=None):
    # If h not all 0/1, choose top n//2 as 1. If h length odd? n even maybe 700.
    target=n//2
    if n odd target? sum must n/2 not integer -> continuous; but prefer even n. We'll use even n.
    vals=h.copy()
    # threshold: if already binary sum ok return
    if np.all((vals==0)|(vals==1)): return vals.astype(np.int8)
    order=np.argpartition(-vals, target)[:target]
    b=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int8); b[order]=1
    return b
```
For n equal len parent maybe 700 even. For other n choose even.

Projection exact: Let's implement robust `project_box_sum`.
```
def project_box_sum(y, S):
    y = np.asarray(y,dtype=float).copy()
    n=len(y)
    # Exact l2 projection via bisection on lambda
    def f(lam): return np.clip(y-lam,0,1).sum() - S
    # lam in [min(y)-1, max(y)+1]? Since increasing y decreases sum.
    lo = y.min() - 1.0
    hi = y.max() + 1.0
    # f(lo)>0? if lam small, y-lam high => sum high; f(hi)<0.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       if np.clip(y-mid,0,1).sum() > S: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    return np.clip(y-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
```
60 iterations * vector operations. Could reduce to 40. Need ensure lo/hi bracket. If S between 0,n, y in [0,1], lo=min(y)-1 <= min, at lam=min-1, y-lam≥1? For min y maybe 0, lam=-1 => clip sum n >S (if S=n/2). hi=max+1 =>0. Good. 40 enough for n 700 tolerance 1e-10. We can call many times; 40*700 length. For 1000 calls ->28M, okay. Could optimize by first mean correction: if within bounds after `y -= (y.sum()-S)/n`, return y. Do that:
```
y = y.copy()
corr = (S-y.sum())/n
y2 = y + corr
if y2.min()>=0 and y2.max()<=1: return y2
# else bisection
```

```
def smooth_opt(h_in, n, deadline, start_alpha=20, max_alpha=50000, iters_per_alpha=??):
    h = project(h_in,n/2)
    best = h.copy(); best_c=exact
    for alpha in alphas:
      if time>deadline break
      F,c,grad = smooth_info
      step = 1.0/alpha? 
      momentum=0.0? maybe use v=0
      for it in range(max_iters):
        if time>deadline break
        F_old, c, grad = smooth_info(h,n,alpha)
        gt = grad - grad.mean()
        gnorm = np.linalg.norm(gt)
        if gnorm < 1e-10: break
        # try step sizes
        accepted=False
        step_cur = step
        for _ in range(12):
            h_new = project_box_sum(h - step_cur*gt, n/2)
            F_new, c_new = smooth_value(h_new,n,alpha)
            if F_new <= F_old - 1e-4*step_cur*gnorm*gnorm:
                h=h_new; F=F_new; accepted=True; step=min(step*1.1, some_max)
                # update exact best if improved
                break
            step_cur *= 0.5
            if step_cur < 1e-6: break
        if not accepted:
            # maybe try smaller fixed step without Armijo? or move to stochastic active?
            step *= 0.5
            if step < 1e-8: break
      # after each alpha, exact c, if lower best update
```

Let's define `exact_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline)`:
```
h=project(h)
c=correlate
best_h=h; best_c=best
for pass in range(max_passes):
 if time>deadline: break

 for p in range(n):
   if time>deadline: break
   if h[p]<1e-9 or h[p]>1-1e-9: continue
   x=solve_coord(c,h,p,n)
   if abs(x-h[p])>1e-12:
      update c, h; improved=True
 # projection
 if improved:
   h2=project_box_sum(h,n/2)
   c=correlate(h2)
   cval=c.max()*dx
   if cval<best_c-1e-10:
      best_h=h2.copy(); best_c=cval
   h=h2
 else: break
return best_h,best_c
```

Need solve_coord diagonal condition correct. Let's write code.
```
def solve_coord(c,h,p,n):
    L=2*n-1; diag=n-1; x0=h[p]
    a=np.zeros(L)
    # first left
    if p>0:
       a[n-1-p:n-1] += 1.0 - h[:p]
    r=n-1-p
    if r>0:
       a[n:n+r] += 1.0 - h[p+1:p+r+1]
    # second left (negative d)
    if r>0:
       # d=-s, s=1..r: indices n-1-s -> n-1-r .. n-2 => start n-1-r = p? since r=n-1-p -> p? Actually start p? n-1-r = p. For length r, indices p .. p+r-1 = n-2. h[p+1:p+r+1]
       a[p:n-1] += -h[p+1:p+r+1]  # start p? if p=1,r=1 => a[1:2] length1 OK. If p=0,r=2 => a[0:2] length2; but d range -2,-1. Yes.
    # second right positive d
    if p>0:
       # d=1..p indices n..n+p; h indices p-1 down to 0 => h[p-1:-1:-1]
       a[n:n+p] += -h[p-1:p-p-1:-1]
```
Check start for second left: indices for d=-r to -1: l=n-1-r = p, to n-2 inclusive length r. Correct. For p=0,r=n-1: second left indices 0..n-2, but d=negative from -(n-1) to -1; yes. First left none. First right indices n..2n-2, length r. Second right none. Union all lags. For p=0, a diag should0. But first right d=1..n-1, second left d=-(n-1)..-1. Good.
Need make not include diag? Slices exclude diag n-1: first right starts n, first left ends n-1 exclusive; second left ends n-1 exclusive; second right starts n. Good.
Set a[diag]=0.
`base = c - x0*a`

```
def project_box_sum(y,S):
 y=np.clip(np.asarray(y,float), -1e6,1e6)? no.
 y=y.copy()
 corr=(S-y.sum())/y.size
 y2=y+corr
 if y2.min()>=-1e-12 and y2.max()<=1+1e-12:
    return np.clip(y2,0,1)
 lo=min(y.min()-1.0, -1.0?) hi=max(y.max()+1.0, 2.0?)
 # Actually if y may outside, use lo=y.min()-1, hi=y.max()+1.
 for _ in range(50):
   mid=(lo+hi)/2
   if np.clip(y-mid,0,1).sum() > S: lo=mid
   else: hi=mid
 z=np.clip(y-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
 # ensure sum exactly? Could adjust mean small within bounds.
 if z.sum() < S:
   room=1-z; tot=room.sum(); if tot>0: z=np.clip(z+(S-z.sum())*room/tot,0,1)
 else: similar
 return z
```

Implement greedy binary:
```
def binary_improve(hb,n,deadline,seed=0):
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 h=hb.astype(np.int8).copy()
 c=np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full').astype(np.int32)
 dx=2.0/n
 best=max
 no_improve=0
 while time<deadline:
   cm=int(c.max())
   # scan max lags
   max_idx=np.flatnonzero(c==cm)
   if len==0: break
   improved=False
   # deterministic over max_idx
   for idx in max_idx:
     d=idx-(n-1)
     if d==0: continue
     if d>0:
       a=h[:-d]; b=h[d:]
       pairs=np.flatnonzero((a==1)&(b==0))
       if len(pairs)==0: continue
       for pos in pairs:
          i=int(pos); j=int(pos+d)
          apply_flip(c,h,i,True); apply_flip(c,h,j,False)
          nm=int(c.max())
          if nm < cm:
             best...; improved=True; break
          else:
             apply_flip(c,h,j,True); apply_flip(c,h,i,False)
       if improved: break
     else:
       s=-d
       a=h[s:]; b=h[:-s]
       pairs=np.flatnonzero((a==1)&(b==0))
       for pos in pairs:
          i=int(pos+s); j=int(pos)
          apply_flip...
   if improved:
      no_improve=0; continue
   # random tries
   no_improve += 1
   if no_improve > 50 and ...? maybe accept small increase with temperature
   # high lags >=cm-1
   high_idx=np.flatnonzero(c>=cm-1)
   tried=0
   improved_random=False
   while tried<200 and time<deadline:
      idx=high_idx[rng.randint(len(high_idx))]
      ... sample pair
      try move; if nm<cm accept; elif nm==cm and rng.random()<0.1: accept? equal may allow escape but best unchanged; can accept random equal to diversify (keep). But accepting equal doesn't lower best; may help. We can accept equal with low probability if no improvement.
      tried++
   if improved_random: no_improve=0
   else: break
return h,best
```
If accept equal, c max same but pattern changes; need track h/c. Could help. But for final best, only lower accepted. We can allow equal after deterministic fails.

However if c is int32 and max cm, swapping pair from max lag may often raise another max to cm (same), not improve. Equal moves could explore. We can accept up to limited equal moves.

```
def project_box_sum(y,S):
 y=np.asarray(y,float).copy()
 if y.size==0: return y
 if abs(y.sum()-S)<1e-11 and y.min()>=-1e-11 and y.max()<=1+1e-11: return y
 if y.sum()>S:
   # maybe scale? not.
 corr=(S-y.sum())/y.size
 y2=y+corr
 if y2.min()>=-1e-12 and y2.max()<=1+1e-12:
   return np.clip(y2,0.0,1.0)
 lo=y.min()-1.0
 hi=y.max()+1.0
 if lo< -2.0: lo=-2.0
 if hi>2.0: hi=2.0
 for _ in range(35):
   mid=.5*(lo+hi)
   if np.clip(y-mid,0,1).sum()>S: lo=mid
   else: hi=mid
 z=np.clip(y-.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
 # small fix
 diff=S-z.sum()
 if diff>0:
   room=1-z; tot=room.sum();
   if tot>1e-15: z=np.clip(z+diff*room/tot,0,1)
 elif diff<0:
   tot=z.sum();
   if tot>1e-15: z=z*(1+diff/tot) # or proportional reduce
   z=np.clip(z,0,1)
 return z
```

```
def exact_cd(h_in,n,max_passes,deadline):
 h=project_box_sum(h_in,n/2)
 c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
 dx=2.0/n; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.max()*dx
 improved_rounds=0
 for _ in range(max_passes):
   if time>deadline: break
   changed=False
   for p in range(n):
     if time>deadline: break
     x,a=solve_coord(c,h,p,n)
     if abs(x-h[p])>1e-12:
       delta=x-h[p]
       c += a*delta
       c[n-1] += (1-2*h[p])*delta - delta*delta
       h[p]=x
       changed=True
   if not changed: break
   h2=project_box_sum(h,n/2)
   c2=np.correlate(h2,1-h2,'full')
   val=c2.max()*dx
   if val < best_c -1e-11:
      best_h=h2.copy(); best_c=val; improved_rounds=0
   else:
      improved_rounds +=1
      if improved_rounds>=4: break
   h=h2; c=c2
 return best_h,best_c
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np

def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx)

def project_box_sum(y, S):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float).copy()
    n = y.size
    if n == 0:
        return y
    if abs(float(y.sum()) - float(S)) <= 1e-11 and y.min() >= -1e-11 and y.max() <= 1.0 + 1e-11:
        return y
    corr = (float(S) - float(y.sum())) / n
    y2 = y + corr
    if y2.min() >= -1e-12 and y2.max() <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
        return np.clip(y2, 0.0, 1.0)
    lo = y.min() - 1.0
    hi = y.max() + 1.0
    if lo < -2.0:
        lo = -2.0
    if hi > 2.0:
        hi = 2.0
    for _ in range(35):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum() > S:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    z = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = float(S) - float(z.sum())
    if diff > 1e-13:
        room = 1.0 - z
        tot = room.sum()
        if tot > 1e-15:
            z = np.clip(z + diff * room / tot, 0.0, 1.0)
    elif diff < -1e-13:
        tot = z.sum()
        if tot > 1e-15:
            z = np.clip(z + diff * z / tot, 0.0, 1.0)
    return z

def smooth_value(c, alpha):
    cm = float(c.max())
    if alpha <= 1e-12:
        return cm
    z = alpha * (c - cm)
    s = float(np.sum(np.exp(z)))
    return cm + np.log(s) / alpha

def smooth_info(h, n, alpha):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    cm = float(c.max())
    if alpha <= 1e-12:
        w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / c.size)
    else:
        w = np.exp(alpha * (c - cm))
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(c.size)
            w[np.argmax(c)] = 1.0
        else:
            w = w / s
    wr = w[::-1]
    a = 1.0 - 2.0 * h
    wsum = wr + w
    wdiff = wr - w
    sl = (n - 1, 2 * n - 1)
    g = 0.5 * (np.convolve(a, wsum, mode="full")[sl] +
               np.convolve(np.ones(n), wdiff, mode="full")[sl])
    return smooth_value(c, alpha), g, c

def smooth_run(h_in, n, deadline):
    S = 0.5 * n
    h = project_box_sum(h_in, S)
    c0 = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(c0.max()) * (2.0 / n)
    alphas = [20.0, 80.0, 320.0, 1280.0, 5120.0, 20000.0]
    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        h = project_box_sum(best_h, S)
        step = min(0.25, 1.0 / (3.0 * alpha))
        for _ in range(36):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            F0, g, _ = smooth_info(h, n, alpha)
            gt = g - float(g.mean())
            ng = float(np.linalg.norm(gt))
            if ng < 1e-12:
                break
            cur_step = step
            accepted = False
            trial_best_h = None
            trial_best_F = float("inf")
            trial_best_c = None
            for _ in range(9):
                hn = project_box_sum(h - cur_step * gt, S)
                cn = np.correlate(hn, 1.0 - hn, mode="full")
                Fn = smooth_value(cn, alpha)
                ex = float(cn.max()) * (2.0 / n)
                if Fn < trial_best_F:
                    trial_best_F = Fn
                    trial_best_h = hn
                    trial_best_c = cn
                    trial_best_x = ex
                if np.isfinite(Fn) and Fn <= F0 - 1e-4 * cur_step * ng * ng:
                    h = hn
                    if trial_best_x < best_c:
                        best_h = hn.copy()
                        best_c = trial_best_x
                    if step < 0.25:
                        step = min(0.25, step * 1.15)
                    accepted = True
                    break
                cur_step *= 0.5
                if cur_step < 1e-10:
                    break
            if not accepted:
                if trial_best_h is not None and trial_best_F < F0 - 1e-12:
                    h = trial_best_h
                    if trial_best_x < best_c:
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        best_c = trial_best_x
                    accepted = True
                else:
                    step *= 0.5
                    if step < 1e-10:
                        break
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        ccur = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        cval = float(ccur.max()) * (2.0 / n)
        if cval > best_c + 1e-9:
            h = best_h.copy()
    h = project_box_sum(best_h, S)
    return h, compute_c5(h, n)

def solve_coord(c, h, p, n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    diag = n - 1
    x0 = float(h[p])
    a = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    if p > 0:
        a[n - 1 - p:n - 1] += 1.0 - h[:p]
    r = n - 1 - p
    if r > 0:
        a[n:n + r] += 1.0 - h[p + 1:p + r + 1]
        a[p:n - 1] -= h[p + 1:p + r + 1]
    if p > 0:
        a[n:n + p] -= h[p - 1:p - p - 1:-1]
    a[diag] = 0.0
    base = c - x0 * a
    bdiag = float(c[diag] - x0 + x0 * x0)
    line = np.ones(L, dtype=bool)
    line[diag] = False
    tol = 1e-14
    zero = line & (np.abs(a) <= tol)
    pos = line & (a > tol)
    neg = line & (a < -tol)
    lo = 0.0
    if np.any(zero):
        lo = float(base[zero].max())
    hi = float(c.max())
    if not (lo < hi):
        hi = max(hi, lo + 1e-12)
    def diag_ok(Lv):
        disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * (Lv - bdiag)
        if disc < -1e-12:
            return False
        if disc < 0.0:
            return True
        r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - np.sqrt(disc))
        r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(disc))
        ok = False
        if r1 > 0.0:
            ri = min(1.0, r1)
            if x_lo <= ri and x_hi >= 0.0:
                ok = True
        if r2 < 1.0:
            ll = max(0.0, r2)
            if x_hi >= 1.0 and x_lo <= ll:
                ok = True
        if not ok and r2 > 0.0 and r1 < 1.0:
            if not (x_lo > r1 and x_hi < r2):
                ok = True
        return ok
    for _ in range(45):
        Lv = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        feasible = True
        if feasible and np.any(zero) and float(base[zero].max()) > Lv + 1e-12:
            feasible = False
        x_lo, x_hi = 0.0, 1.0
        if feasible and np.any(pos):
            x_hi = min(x_hi, float(np.min((Lv - base[pos]) / a[pos])))
        if feasible and np.any(neg):
            x_lo = max(x_lo, float(np.max((Lv - base[neg]) / a[neg])))
        if feasible:
            if x_hi < 0.0 or x_lo > 1.0:
                feasible = False
            else:
                x_lo = max(0.0, x_lo)
                x_hi = min(1.0, x_hi)
                if x_lo > x_hi + 1e-12:
                    feasible = False
        if feasible and not diag_ok(Lv):
            feasible = False
        if feasible:
            hi = Lv
        else:
            lo = Lv
    Lv = hi
    x_lo, x_hi = 0.0, 1.0
    if np.any(pos):
        x_hi = min(x_hi, float(np.min((Lv - base[pos]) / a[pos])))
    if np.any(neg):
        x_lo = max(x_lo, float(np.max((Lv - base[neg]) / a[neg])))
    x_lo = max(0.0, x_lo)
    x_hi = min(1.0, x_hi)
    if x_lo > x_hi + 1e-12:
        return x0, a
    disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * (Lv - bdiag)
    if disc < 0.0:
        x = 0.5 * (x_lo + x_hi)
    else:
        r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - np.sqrt(disc))
        r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(disc))
        candidates = []
        if r1 > 0.0:
            ll = max(x_lo, 0.0)
            rr = min(x_hi, min(1.0, r1))
            if rr >= ll - 1e-12:
                candidates.append((ll, rr))
        if r2 < 1.0:
            ll = max(x_lo, max(0.0, r2))
            rr = min(x_hi, 1.0)
            if rr >= ll - 1e-12:
                candidates.append((ll, rr))
        if not candidates:
            x = x0
        else:
            best_len = -1.0
            best_X = None
            for ll, rr in candidates:
                ln = rr - ll
                if ln > best_len:
                    best_len = ln
                    best_X = 0.5 * (ll + rr)
            x = best_X
    return float(np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)), a

def exact_cd(h_in, n, max_passes, deadline):
    S = 0.5 * n
    dx = 2.0 / n
    h = project_box_sum(h_in, S)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(c.max()) * dx
    stall = 0
    diag = n - 1
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        changed = False
        for p in range(n):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            x, a = solve_coord(c, h, p, n)
            old = float(h[p])
            if abs(x - old) > 1e-12:
                delta = x - old
                c += a * delta
                c[diag] += (1.0 - 2.0 * old) * delta - delta * delta
                h[p] = x
                changed = True
        if not changed:
            break
        h2 = project_box_sum(h, S)
        c2 = np.correlate(h2, 1.0 - h2, mode="full")
        val = float(c2.max()) * dx
        if val < best_c - 1e-11:
            best_h = h2.copy()
            best_c = val
            stall = 0
            h = h2
            c = c2
        else:
            stall += 1
            if val > best_c + 1e-6:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
            else:
                h = h2
                c = c2
            if stall >= 4:
                break
    return best_h, compute_c5(best_h, n)

def binary_from_h(h, n):
    S = 0.5 * n
    target = int(round(S))
    hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    if np.all((h == 0) | (h == 1)):
        hb[:] = np.where(h > 0.5, 1, 0).astype(np.int8)
        if hb.sum() == target:
            return hb
    idx = np.argpartition(-h, target)[:target]
    hb[idx] = 1
    return hb

def apply_binary_flip(c, h, p, n, one_to_zero):
    M = max(p, n - 1 - p)
    for d in range(-M, M + 1):
        if d == 0:
            continue
        l = n - 1 + d
        delta = 0
        j = p + d
        if 0 <= j < n:
            delta += 1 - int(h[j])
        i = p - d
        if 0 <= i < n:
            delta -= int(h[i])
        if delta:
            if one_to_zero:
                c[l] -= delta
            else:
                c[l] += delta
    h[p] = 1 - int(h[p])

def binary_improve(hb_in, n, deadline, seed=0):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    S = 0.5 * n
    target = int(round(S))
    h = hb_in.astype(np.int8).copy()
    if h.sum() != target:
        h = binary_from_h(h.astype(float), n)
    c = np.asarray(np.correlate(h, 1 - h, mode="full"), dtype=np.int32)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(c.max()) * dx
    no_improve = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        cm = int(c.max())
        improved = False
        max_idx = np.flatnonzero(c == cm)
        if max_idx.size == 0:
            break
        for oi in range(max_idx.size):
            idx = int(max_idx[oi])
            d = idx - (n - 1)
            if d == 0:
                continue
            if d > 0:
                aa = h[:-d]
                bb = h[d:]
                pairs = np.flatnonzero((aa == 1) & (bb == 0))
            else:
                s = -d
                aa = h[s:]
                bb = h[:-s]
                pairs = np.flatnonzero((aa == 1) & (bb == 0))
            for q in pairs:
                if d > 0:
                    i = int(q)
                    j = i + d
                else:
                    s = -d
                    j = int(q)
                    i = j + s
                apply_binary_flip(c, h, i, n, True)
                apply_binary_flip(c, h, j, n, False)
                nm = int(c.max())
                if nm < cm:
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    best_c = float(nm) * dx
                    improved = True
                    break
                apply_binary_flip(c, h, j, n, True)
                apply_binary_flip(c, h, i, n, False)
            if improved:
                break
        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            continue
        no_improve += 1
        if no_improve > 60:
            break
        high_idx = np.flatnonzero(c >= cm - 1)
        if high_idx.size == 0:
            break
        for _ in range(160):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            idx = int(high_idx[rng.randint(high_idx.size)])
            d = idx - (n - 1)
            if d == 0:
                continue
            if d > 0:
                pairs = np.flatnonzero((h[:-d] == 1) & (h[d:] == 0))
            else:
                s = -d
                pairs = np.flatnonzero((h[s:] == 1) & (h[:-s] == 0))
            if pairs.size == 0:
                continue
            q = int(pairs[rng.randint(pairs.size)])
            if d > 0:
                i = q
                j = q + d
            else:
                s = -d
                j = q
                i = q + s
            apply_binary_flip(c, h, i, n, True)
            apply_binary_flip(c, h, j, n, False)
            nm = int(c.max())
            if nm < cm:
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_c = float(nm) * dx
                no_improve = 0
                break
            if nm == cm and rng.random() < 0.25:
                no_improve = 0
                break
            apply_binary_flip(c, h, j, n, True)
            apply_binary_flip(c, h, i, n, False)
    return best_h.astype(float), best_c

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        n0 = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        n0 = 700
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5)
    if n0 % 2 == 1:
        n0 = max(4, 2 * (n0 // 2))
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(h0))
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n0)
        h0 = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h0)
    if n0 > 950:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n0)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, 950)
        h0 = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h0)
        n0 = 950
    h0 = np.clip(h0, 0.0, 1.0)
    S = 0.5 * n0
    h0 = project_box_sum(h0, S)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n0)

    cands = []
    base_cands = [h0, 1.0 - h0, h0[::-1], 1.0 - h0[::-1], binary_from_h(h0, n0).astype(float)]
    for s in (0.04, 0.10, 0.18, 0.35):
        base_cands.append(h0 + s * rng.randn(n0))
    for hc in base_cands:
        try:
            hf = project_box_sum(hc, S)
            cc = compute_c5(hf, n0)
            cands.append((cc, hf))
        except Exception:
            pass
    for i in range(len(cands)):
        mj = i
        for j in range(i + 1, len(cands)):
            if cands[j][0] < cands[i][0]:
                mj = j
        if mj != i:
            cands[i], cands[mj] = cands[mj], cands[i]

    smooth_deadline = min(deadline - 380.0, t0 + 420.0)
    for idx in range(min(3, len(cands))):
        cc, hc = cands[idx]
        if time.time() > smooth_deadline - 60.0:
            break
        if cc > best_c + 0.12:
            break
        dl = min(deadline - 380.0, time.time() + 150.0)
        try:
            hs, cs = smooth_run(hc, n0, dl)
            if cs < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hs, cs
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, S)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n0)

    bin_deadline = min(deadline - 180.0, time.time() + 320.0)
    bin_starts = [binary_from_h(best_h, n0)]
    if len(cands) > 1:
        bin_starts.append(binary_from_h(cands[1][1], n0))
    if len(cands) > 2:
        bin_starts.append(binary_from_h(cands[2][1], n0))
    for bi, hs in enumerate(bin_starts):
        if time.time() > bin_deadline - 30.0:
            break
        dl = min(deadline - 180.0, time.time() + 140.0)
        try:
            hb, cb = binary_improve(hs, n0, dl, seed + 101 + bi)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_h = hb
                best_c = cb
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, S)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n0)

    if time.time() < deadline - 40.0:
        try:
            he, ce = exact_cd(best_h, n0, 24, deadline - 20.0)
            if ce < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = he, ce
        except Exception:
            pass

    remaining = deadline - 8.0 - time.time()
    if remaining > 60.0:
        try:
            hs, cs = smooth_run(best_h, n0, deadline - 8.0)
            if cs < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hs, cs
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, S)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n0)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(n0)
```
